## Project cognition bootstrap capsule (2026-09-15)

This is a derived Project-level map, not a competing Source of Truth. Source, tests, ADRs and the architecture documents remain authoritative for their domains.

- Product direction: preserve Code-OSS/AgentHost/Sessions platform value while moving Nimora product semantics toward **Thin Core + Nimora-owned Task/Project/Mission domains + Worker Adapters + Capability Layer + modular Gateway**.
- Domain hierarchy: Project is long-lived; Mission owns one bounded historical purpose; the existing TaskRuntime remains the durable work-state substrate during incremental migration; WorkerSession is replaceable compute; Capability is executable ability with risk/approval/retry semantics.
- Durable ownership: `ProjectStore` owns Project identity/metadata plus the Phase 5 bounded Proposal / explicit Human Confirmation / Committed Decision governance journal; it still owns no Mission membership, Worker lifecycle, collaboration graph, provider transcript or delivery ledger. `TaskRuntime` owns Mission identity/membership, work state, Worker refs, executions, grants, artifacts and Handoff substrate; `WorkerSessionManager` owns active Worker routing/lifecycle; `MissionCollaborationStore` owns only Phase 3 cross-Mission relation/exchange facts.
- Collaboration law: full provider transcripts are not Project memory or a collaboration protocol. Cross-Mission continuity uses bounded durable state, evidence, exchanges, artifacts and Handoffs.
- Cognition/Practice law: Cognition forms current best understanding; Practice changes/measures reality and may invalidate that understanding with Problem/Evidence; plans are revisable when evidence changes.
- Human deliberation law: discussion is non-binding until `Draft → Proposed → Human Confirmed → Committed`; ordinary autonomous engineering does not wait for human silence/approval.
- Transport boundary: Bridge/Gateway/WebMCP move requests/results and host browser/external transports; they do not own Project planning or become the Project brain.
- Core boundary: new Project/Mission/Cognition/Practice product semantics stay Nimora-owned unless a narrow generic Core hook is demonstrably necessary.
- Safety invariant: capability execution and result delivery remain distinct; transport uncertainty never authorizes blind re-execution of side effects.
- Mission Work proof chain: Phase 0 established repository-owned architectural/baseline truth; Phase 1 proved durable Project identity + additive Mission metadata without a second runtime; Phase 2 proved explicit Mission death, bounded final Handoff and disposable Worker lifecycle; Phase 3 independently proved durable bounded cross-Mission graph/exchange facts without transcript inheritance or ownership duplication; Phase 4 independently proved the bounded Reality-driven Cognition⇄Practice feedback loop, same-Practice continuation and restart with replacement Worker without a delivery ledger or transcript inheritance.
- Current development authority: **Phases 3–10 are CLOSED / COMPLETE and their Phase-scoped Cognition/Practice/Verifier Missions have exhausted their authority.** The active Phase 11 Cognition remains the Phase-11 planning/reconciliation authority. `P11-HD-01` remains Human Confirmed + Committed. **Repairs WO#1A–#1K are accepted; the same Repair WO#1L remains authorized for continuation/rework on the same persistent Phase 11 Practice Mission.** Practice must retain the stable logical occurrence/result-identity work that passed focused gates, but correct the real DeepSeek `data-virtual-list-item-key` domain/order model so current real negative keys do not make the pre-send boundary invalid and historical replay still remains fenced. It must add an exact real-key-domain regression, independently justify whichever ordering subset/floor it uses, and keep fail-closed semantics for genuinely ambiguous/untrustworthy identity. It must not run a real Coordinator/ChatGPT provider turn, replay DeepSeek v2, resume final-liveness-004/rwv-006, redesign Mission-native MCP/WorkerSession lifecycle/WO#1K poll liveness/WO#1J ChatGPT submit, self-accept R12/WO#1L, create R13/WO#1M, start WO#2 or launch Fresh Independent Verification.
- Machine Coordinator reality: the Phase 6 explicit-command bridge is now implementation-complete for its authorized scope. An ordinary disposable Coordinator Worker executes exact Cognition-authored inspect/ensure/problem-route/transport/completion commands without planning authority; Work Order/fact/feedback/already-Committed Decision transport stays bounded and provider-neutral; replacement Worker continuity does not replace the Coordination Mission; UNKNOWN never creates resend authority; completion is Cognition-authored and prose-free; the final Worker turn reaches trustworthy terminal and releases its send lease before lifecycle mutation; root/Coordinator death, required Handoffs and Worker retirement remain owned by Phase 2 finalization; Task child admission and collaboration blocker admission are both linearized against the completion boundary; partial Coordinator-first/root-active crash state recovers deterministically without a pending-action journal.

## Coordinator continuity

- This section is a development-routing compatibility protocol, not a grant of planning authority to the product Coordinator. Where an active Phase Cognition Mission has explicit planning authority, that Phase-specific authority wins. **Phase 10 Cognition has completed and exhausted its authority; Project-level Cognition / 总文 has now delegated Phase 11 in-Phase planning authority to the fresh Phase 11 Cognition / Architecture Mission.** The manual bridge and product Coordinator do not infer decomposition, formation, provider-policy or next-Phase authority from this section.
- The currently authorized routing actor continues transport/execution autonomously within explicit instructions; this does **not** grant product or development bridge actors independent Mission-planning authority. A Worker terminal state is routing input, not proof that the owning Cognition scope has reached its semantic completion boundary.
- Routing autonomy is authority-scoped. Phase Cognition may route work inside its assigned Phase; it may not infer authority to create the next Phase from its own closure. Project-level Cognition owns cross-Phase reconciliation and next-Phase dispatch.
- After every Worker terminal result, the result returns to the Cognition scope that owns the semantic decision. That Cognition scope reconciles repository facts and determines the next valid route **within its authority**; the bridge/Coordinator then transports or executes the resulting explicit route without inventing a decomposition of its own. At a Phase terminal boundary, Phase Cognition returns a Final Phase Handoff + `PROJECT COGNITION REPORT` upward; Project-level Cognition then decides the cross-Phase route.
- Mission completion, test PASS/FAIL, a required Repair or Verification, a known next Mission, or a Phase transition are not reasons to stop.
- Human silence is not an approval requirement for ordinary autonomous engineering work.
- Pause only for a genuine strategic/value/irreversible decision, an external authorization or user-only action, absence of any safe evidence-derived next Mission, or an unreliable Coordinator session. An unreliable Coordinator must produce a bounded Coordinator Handoff instead of silently stopping.
- The Coordinator terminates only when its assigned root coordination Mission reaches its completion criteria.

## Coordinator conversation economy

- Existing Worker conversations are scarce Mission resources and must be reused by default. Retry, test failure, defect discovery, unfinished checklist work, temporary network/browser interruption, continuation messaging, elapsed time, or token use do not justify a replacement conversation.
- Before creating a Worker conversation, apply the CHAT CREATION GATE: verify that it is a genuinely new historical Mission, an explicit independent-verification boundary, or an actually unusable/corrupted session. If the current conversation can safely continue, creating another conversation is prohibited.
- Related defects remain consolidated in the active Repair Mission. After an interruption, resume the exact surviving Worker conversation and preserve its repository state.
- Fresh conversations are for Mission-history or epistemic-independence boundaries, not convenience. Keep each substantial Phase to a small number of coherent conversations.
- `MISSION_WORK_ROADMAP.md` now makes the Mission Creation Gate explicit and adds a human-confirmed Phase 3 operating experiment: Phase-level Cognition owns planning authority, Phase-level Practice may execute multiple Work Orders and wait between them, ordinary Practice results return to Cognition rather than defining their own successor Mission, and the human/technical bridge only transports structured commands/results. This protocol is deliberately revisable from Phase 3 evidence.

## 2026-09-28 — Human-authorized Current Route restart hardening / source implementation staged

- Human product direction: routine Cloudflare Quick Tunnel URL rotation must be absorbed by Nimora/ShunCode rather than requiring the user to edit or recreate the ChatGPT connector after every computer or runtime restart.
- Source implementation adds an opt-in Current Route Relay transport indirection. The stable ChatGPT MCP URL points at one Cloudflare Worker plus SQLite-backed Durable Object; ShunCode publishes the current exact direct Mission-native MCP URL to that Relay after _shuncode.projectFormation.prepareNativeMcp and again whenever BridgeManager reports a new public URL after tunnel recovery.
- Architectural ownership is unchanged: the Relay stores only the current transport pointer. MissionNativeMcpBindingService, Phase-8 materialization and grants, Worker ownership, execution ledger and replay rules remain authoritative. No global or base MCP authority is introduced.
- Security is capability-preserving: provider traffic is exposed only at /r/<ROUTE_PATH_TOKEN>/mcp; route mutation and readback additionally require independent ROUTE_UPDATE_SECRET. The update secret is stored in VS Code SecretStorage. Root /mcp is not exposed by the reference Relay.
- When configured, prepareNativeMcp.publicUrl becomes the verified stable Relay MCP URL while directPublicUrl retains the exact current tunnel/Mission endpoint. Without Relay configuration the previous direct URL behavior remains unchanged.
- Added command and configuration surface: shuncode.bridge.configureCurrentRouteRelay, shuncode.bridge.getCurrentRouteRelayStatus, shuncode.bridge.clearCurrentRouteRelay, and shuncode.bridge.currentRouteRelayUrl.
- Deterministic validation PASS: typecheck-shuncode; test-shuncode-current-route-relay; test-shuncode-phase11-native-mcp; test-shuncode-phase11-production-routing; test-shuncode-bridge-startup-decoupling. The dedicated Relay gate covers URL rotation and deduplication, read-after-write fail-closed behavior, hidden root /mcp, admin auth, MCP POST forwarding and mcp-session-id preservation.
- compile-shuncode was attempted but the active Windows Extension Host currently holds extensions/shuncode/runtime/bin/shuncode_process_metadata.node; the build script clean step returned EPERM before bundling. This is an active-runtime file lock, not a TypeScript or test failure; the live host was intentionally not killed just to satisfy the build gate.
- Reference deployment and operating contract: docs/architecture/CURRENT_ROUTE_RELAY.md; Worker source: tools/nimora-current-route-relay/worker.mjs.
- Authority note: this is user-authorized product hardening and source staging only. It does not mark R18, WO#1, or Phase 11 closed, does not mint a successor Final Proof identity, and does not substitute for the current Phase 11 Cognition/Practice acceptance flow.

## 2026-09-28 — P0 reliability foundation / observation source implemented and preservation-regression verified

- P0 audit conclusion: today's Worker Conversation Lifecycle, Current Route Relay and Mission Parallel Orchestration already solve the core context-renewal, URL-churn and cross-Mission fan-out problems. This tranche therefore does **not** create a second lifecycle/recovery/scheduler stack; it adds the missing provider-neutral observation and reliability-test seams around those existing owners.
- New `src/mission-observation-event.ts` defines a bounded non-owning observation envelope with Project/root/Mission scope, domain/operational/telemetry kind, canonical source-record reference, correlation/causation/occurrence seams and optional Worker identity. TaskRuntime / ProjectStore / MissionCollaborationStore projection helpers point back to canonical events/exchanges and do not copy their canonical payloads.
- Observation facts are bounded primitive diagnostics only. Prompt/transcript/reasoning/message/raw/HTML/DOM-style fact keys are rejected, including camelCase forms. The first smoke run found a `providerTranscript` camelCase bypass in the initial matcher; source was repaired and the regression now passes. Unknown observation owners also fail closed at runtime.
- New `src/browser-worker-observation.ts` defines provider-neutral transport/page/conversation/turn/auth/capability dimensions and derives ready / busy / waiting-human / recovering / connection-lost / page-drift / conversation-drift / capability-drift / settlement-unknown / unavailable / retired. Runtime enum validation fails closed instead of allowing an unknown JS/provider value to accidentally derive ready.
- Safety boundary: browser/provider `turnSettlement` is observation only and is **not** external side-effect settlement or retry authority. Connection loss during a running turn remains active; durable Worker retirement dominates transient browser state; observation never finalizes a Mission, retires/replaces a Worker, grants capability authority or mutates Project truth.
- New `scripts/shuncode-p0-reliability-foundation-smoke.mts` is the first common Reliability Lab seam. Current-checkout PASS gates: P0 reliability foundation; Worker conversation lifecycle; Mission parallel orchestration; WorkerSessionManager; WebWorkerAdapter; Current Route Relay; Phase 11 native MCP; Phase 11 production routing; strict targeted TypeScript check; typecheck-shuncode. VS Code diagnostics for both new source files report 0 errors / 0 warnings.
- MCP audit: the checkout already uses official `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk ^1.30.0`. Upstream 2026-07-28 removes modern protocol-level sessions/initialize semantics and the official TypeScript v2 SDK is the migration path. No custom MCP stack is being invented. ## 2026-09-28 — Phase 11 closure diagnosis / no authority change

- Immediate state: Phase 11 is **not** currently blocked on an accepted-but-unrepaired source defect. R18 source repair is accepted and repaired-runtime PRE-SEND is accepted. The live WO#1 gate is now the one-shot Final Proof `025/027`, still RESERVED / UNCONSUMED, followed by Phase 11 Cognition final acceptance.
- Important scope correction: passing `025/027` can close **WO#1**, not Phase 11 by itself. The persisted historical Phase 11 plan still lists WO#2, WO#3 and WO#4, but those entries must **not** be treated as untouched future work. Current source already substantially subsumes WO#2 and the core of WO#3: `ProjectFormationApplicationService` accepts assignment `constraints/preferences`, exposes exact current-Worker retirement and restart-orphan recovery, and the formation orchestration smoke explicitly proves fresh replacement on the SAME persistent Mission; Phase 10 is independently CLOSED / COMPLETE with Project Formation -> root/Coordinator -> assignment/restart behavior verified. The remaining WO#3-shaped gap is the normal user-facing ShunCode entry surface: current extension wiring exposes internal `_shuncode.projectFormation.*` commands, while the contributed command surface contains no public Project/Formation command and the earlier Phase 11 checkpoint correctly recorded that normal native Chat bypasses Project Formation. Therefore post-WO#1 work must begin with a fresh scope reconciliation rather than blindly re-executing historical WO#2/WO#3.
- Why WO#1 took so long: it became the first full production integration proof across real ChatGPT + DeepSeek browser state, provider-native identity, restart/orphan ownership, WebMCP control, Mission-native MCP, exact occurrence/replay authority and actual workspace mutation. Real one-shot runs repeatedly exposed latent boundaries that synthetic/fake-adapter tests had not exercised.
- The defect history is not one repeating bug. It spans distinct classes: provider conversation/retirement identity (R1), exact composer observation (R2), same-Mission retirement/replacement surface and restart orphan convergence (R3/R4/R8), WebMCP bounded liveness and safe internal browser-control execution (R5/R6/R7), send-abandon/convergence ownership (R9), exact submit/browser behavior (R10+), occurrence/result identity and provider-history virtualization (R12), provider admission/occurrence ordering (R16), trusted-host command authority across model text retranscription (R17), and current-response terminal truth / active Phase-8 authority lifetime (R18).
- The proof process intentionally amplifies the cost of each defect: after semantic invocation begins, the proof pair is permanently consumed regardless of outcome. Any new defect therefore requires preserving terminal evidence, source repair, Cognition acceptance, runtime refresh if needed, canonical retirement of consumed Worker generations, fresh assignment/binding/provider route, PRE-SEND reconciliation and a new zero-history proof pair. This preserves no-replay/exactly-once truth but makes each integration miss operationally expensive.
- A second structural cause is test-surface mismatch. Several deterministic tests historically bypassed real provider transport/DOM behavior. R17 is the clearest example: fake adapters cloned the host-authored structured argument directly, while real DeepSeek WebMCP retranscribed it through provider-visible text and changed formatting. R18 similarly exposed that stable assistant text + non-streaming was not sufficient provider-terminal truth. Final Proof therefore acted as the first test of some real transport semantics instead of only the final acceptance gate.
- A third cause is environment churn being inside the proof envelope: Extension Host generations, browser page generations, retired Worker lineage, Cloudflare Quick Tunnel/public route, provider plugin/app selection, provider-originated MCP sessions and current provider DOM all have to align at one moment. Current Route Relay and the newer lifecycle/observation work reduce this class going forward, but they were added after much of the WO#1 repair history.
- Shortest safe path now: do **not** redesign Phase 11 before `025/027`. Freeze proof-sensitive source, execute the already-authorized one-shot exactly once, preserve terminal evidence, and let Cognition accept/reject WO#1. If WO#1 passes, Project/Phase Cognition should then reconcile the remaining WO#2-#4 plan against newer already-implemented lifecycle/parallel/Formation foundations and explicitly re-scope only where current evidence truly subsumes old work; otherwise continue the remaining Work Orders. Phase 11 must not be marked closed merely because WO#1 passes.
- Diagnostic conclusion: the main problem is not that Nimora's fail-closed rules are too strict; those rules repeatedly prevented duplicate side effects and stale authority. The problem is that **production browser/provider semantics were discovered too late, inside expensive one-shot acceptance runs**. Future phases should move those provider-transport/DOM/lifecycle falsifiers into a stable Reliability Lab before the final one-shot gate.
- This diagnosis changes no current authority, proof identity, Work Order state, source-freeze rule or Phase 11 acceptance condition.
